● Update(CLAUDE.md)
  ⎿  Added 1 line, removed 1 line
      55  - `kinematics/kiwi.py` — `KiwiKinematics`: forward/inverse kine
          matics + wheel-speed ⇄ stepper conversion.
      56  - `motion/velocity_driver.py` — `VelocityDriver`: rate-limits b
          ody velocity (trapezoidal ramp), then commands all 3 wheels in 
          one `run_all`. Ramping in *body* space keeps the wheel-speed ra
          tio constant, so the path holds during accel (L6470's per-devic
          e ACC alone would skew it). `update(dt)` is pure computation — 
          no sleeps; the caller drives the loop.
      57  - `localization/` — `estimator.py` (`DeadReckoning`: `[X, Y, φ]
          `, midpoint integration, pluggable input: commanded speeds / mi
          crosteps / distances) and `grid.py` (`GridCorrector`: snap X/Y 
          to the 180 mm grid with a `max_error` guard).
      58 -- `perception/red_wall.py` — red wall-top detection (two-range 
         -HSV mask → contour centroids).                                 
      58 +- `perception/` — `red_wall.py` (red wall-top detection: two-ra
         +nge HSV mask → contour centroids) and `wall_detect.py` (`WallDe
         +tector`: per-edge ROI red *fraction* → walls present front/back
         +/left/right, then `body_walls_to_maze()` → `Maze`).            
      59  - `solver/maze.py` — `Maze` / `Direction`: 16×16 grid with **sh
          ared-edge walls** (cell A's east wall *is* cell B's west wall, 
          so they can never disagree), outer walls, centre-2×2 goal, `to_
          ascii()`.
      60  - `strategy/`, `app/` — stubs pending M5–M6.
      61  - `config/` — `robot.yaml` / `maze.yaml` + typed loader (`Robot
          Config`, `MazeConfig`).

● Update(CLAUDE.md)
  ⎿  Added 6 lines
      73  - **Config values are calibrated, not nominal**: `wheel_diamete
          r_m` (~0.0463, effective ≠ nominal 48 mm) and `center_to_wheel_
          m` (~0.0460) came from `scripts/calibrate.py`. Don't "fix" them
           back to spec values, and don't hard-code them in tests — read 
          them off `RobotConfig` (see `tests/test_smoke.py`).
      74  - **L6470 unit gotcha**: the Run/speed registers are in **full 
          step/s** (microstepping does not scale speed); Move / positioni
          ng / odometry counts are in **microsteps**. `KiwiKinematics` ex
          poses both (`wheel_speed_to_step_hz` vs `distance_to_microsteps
          `).
      75  - **picamera2 channel order**: `RGB888` frames are byte-order *
          *BGR** for OpenCV (used directly, no conversion); `red_detect -
          -swap-rb` if colors look inverted.
      76 +- **Wall detection is ROI-based, and the ROIs are calibrated to
         + this exact rig** (`perception/wall_detect.py`, `calibrated_roi
         +s()` / `CALIBRATED_RED`). Camera is centre-mounted ~39 cm up, o
         +ne cell fills the 640×480 frame, and **image top = body forward
         + (+x)**. Hard-won specifics — re-check all of these if the moun
         +t, height, or lighting changes:                                
      77 +  - Walls appear as bands **inside** the frame, *not* at its ed
         +ges, so ROIs sit on the bands.                                 
      78 +  - The four red **lattice posts are always visible at the corn
         +ers** (even with no walls) → ROIs sit mid-edge to avoid them.  
      79 +  - The **camera ribbon cable reads as red** in the lower part 
         +of the frame → the BACK ROI is offset left to dodge it (`WallDe
         +tectorConfig.exclude` can mask fixed self-occlusion too).      
      80 +  - The right-hand wall is **shadowed and desaturated**, so `CA
         +LIBRATED_RED` loosens the red thresholds to `s_min=70, v_min=40
         +`; `red_wall`'s stricter defaults miss it entirely.            
      81 +  - Calibrated separation: wall present ≈ 0.34–0.47 red fractio
         +n, absent ≈ 0.00 (threshold 0.15). Re-tune offline with `script
         +s/wall_detect.py --image <photo>` against labelled shots (all-w
         +alls / no-walls / dead-end).                                   
      82  - **`l6470.decode_status()`**: all-`0x0000`/all-`0xFFFF` ⇒ no S
          PI communication (wiring/power/CS), not a real fault; fault bit
          s are active-low; UVLO on the first read after power-up is the 
          normal power-up latch.
      83

● やり残しを片付けました。PR #51 →

  セッションを分ける前にすべきこと

  1. PR #51 をレビュー→マージ ← これが唯一の未処理です。今セッションで得た「壁は
     内側の帯／四隅に格子点ポスト／ケーブルが赤く写る／右壁は暗く s70,v40
     必要」といった再現の難しい実機知見が CLAUDE.md
     に入るので、これを入れてから切るのがおすすめです。
  2. 状態は確認済みでクリーン: 未コミット・未 push なし / open PR は #51 のみ / 
     main は最新。
  3. 引き継ぎは自動なので手作業は不要です:
     - CLAUDE.md（新セッションで自動読み込み）
     - メモリ（krilly-workflow＝PR運用、pi-gpio-fault＝旧PiのGPIO短絡）
     - GitHub の Issue/Project（残: M4 #17、M5 #18–#20、M6 #21–#23）
  4. 任意の後片付け: 使わなくなった m1/bno055-uart ブランチ（UART 版の残置。I2C
     に移行済み）。将来 UART が必要になる可能性がなければ削除して構いません。

  新セッション開始時

  cd /home/tetsuya/repos/krilly
  source .venv/bin/activate
  git pull            # #51 マージ分を取り込む
  pytest -q           # 130 passed を確認（健全性チェック）
  claude              # -c は付けない（新規セッション＝コンテキストをリセット）

  最初のプロンプトはこれだけで十分です:

  ▎ M4 #17（1セル前進・90°ターンの位置連動）を進めてください。

  CLAUDE.md
  とメモリから、座標系・PR運用・実機の校正値・ハマりどころは引き継がれます。#17
  は VelocityDriver＋DeadReckoning（ジャイロ融合）＋GridCorrector＋壁判定を繋い
  で「1セル動く」を作る工程なので、実機（モーター電源・カメラ）を使える状態にし
  ておくと確認まで進めます。

  ▎ 補足: /compact で継続する選択もありますが、M4 #17 
  ▎ は新しい統合フェーズなので、セッションを新規に切る方がコンテキストがクリーン
  ▎ で安全です（引き継ぎ情報はすべて CLAUDE.md・メモリ・Issue 側にあります）。
